The project page's **멤버** line uses the same words ("나만 봐요" instead of "1명이 봐요"). On a computer an open chat's note now reads "AI는 실수할 수 있어요. 중요한 내용은 확인해 주세요." followed by the line, in place of "내 채팅은 나만 볼 수 있어요". API: `Project.alone` (optional) says that nobody but you sees the project through a rule of its folder. The check ignores roles, and it is sent only to the owner, admins and whoever may change the project's members, who can read its rules anyway; anyone else reads the count. In all five languages.
- A ✦ side chat, and any chat where your message quotes someone else's ("Ask Trina about this" does that), now counts as having read your conversations, as a chat that read a room already did: the AI does not move files to the trash or put anything into a skill folder there, asks on a card before it changes a team file, keeps a memory or sends through a connection, and a file it saves to your folder counts as outside content later. Before, only a room the AI read itself counted, so another person's words in a quote could ask for those without the usual card or refusal. A new file in your own folder still needs no card. No API or data change.
- Reactions can be any emoji now, not only the eight. The add-reaction strip has 😭 and a **More emoji** button that opens a picker: search by name in English (in Korean too), "Recently used" first, every emoji by group; on a phone it is a sheet from the long-press menu. The status dialog's emoji button opens the same picker, its 32 first. A message takes up to 20 different emoji (adding yours to one that is there always works); a reaction is always one emoji, never text. Emoji names come from emojibase-data (MIT) and Unicode CLDR (Unicode License v3) and load only when a picker opens.
- Set your status: an emoji, a few words and when it clears (30 minutes to a week, chosen days for leave, or never), from your account menu or, on a phone, the bottom of the ☰ menu. Suggestions start it in one tap (📅 In a meeting, 🎧 Focusing, 🍽️ At lunch, 🤒 Out sick, 🏖️ On leave). Your team sees the emoji after your name in conversations, the member list, the @ list and on your messages, the words on hover, and a DM with you shows one line above the box ("🏖️ Bob: On leave · until Nov 14"); leave that starts later shows only from its first day, and "today" and "this week" end in the team's time zone. Trina sees statuses too, so "who is on leave this week?" works.
- Connectors is one page instead of two tabs, and Admin says whose it is. Each Connectors page lists the connections first, under **Connected**, then **Discover** with Top connectors and Show all, so a page with one connection no longer looks empty and Discover can no longer be missed; with nothing connected yet Discover comes first. The search filters every section and says "nothing matches" once. Admin's menu title is now your team's logo and name ("Acme admin"), and every Admin page says "Applies to everyone in Acme" above its title, the pair of Settings' "Just for you"; page titles stay the same nouns as in Settings (Providers, Connectors, AI apps), and the grey "For the whole team" beside each title is gone. In Settings › Connectors, what your team provides is one block, **From Acme**, above your own: the team's connections you may use, read only, with **Ready**, and the templates shared with you, with **Connect your account**; admins also see the ones not for them (**Off for you**) and a **Manage** link to Admin. It replaces "Team connections" with its Team chip and "From your team". In Admin each team connection and template shows who may use it in one small chip after its name ("Everyone", "Design +2", "3 people", every name in its tooltip), and a template's line no longer repeats it. Back from a connector's page you land on Discover; after adding from Discover, on the list with the new connection. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- Three small information leaks closed for members. Making a team project, the count under the picker no longer counts the owner and admins for a member (naming an admin left the number as it was and naming anyone else added one, which told who the admins are): it counts you and the people and groups you chose and reads "2명과 팀장·관리자가 봐요"; the owner and admins see the count of everyone, as before. The AI's delete card tells a member that people without write access cannot restore the items instead of how many of them there are (that number told how many people can write the folder); the owner and admins still see it. Every server route has a permission matrix case again, and a new route without one now fails the unit tests. API: an optional `adminsApart` in the answers of `POST /api/projects/access-preview` and the share dry run. No data change.
- Admins change a folder's rules from **볼 수 있는 사람** on the Files page: **규칙 바꾸기** opens the folder in Admin › Folder permissions' edit panel, and closing or saving it comes back to the folder in Files with its panel open again ("파일로 돌아가기"). Not on a team project's own folder (its members are changed on its page) or a personal folder. On a team project's folder the panel has **멤버** (the members dialog) for whoever may change them, and says who does to anyone else. Five languages; no API change.
- 파일 화면에서 폴더를 누가 보는지 바로 알 수 있어요. The Files page now says who can see the folder you are in, at the end of the breadcrumbs: "6명이 봐요", "팀 전체가 봐요", "팀장과 관리자만" or, in your own folder, "나만 봐요". Click it, or **볼 수 있는 사람** in a folder's ⋯ menu: admins see everyone with access by name, their access and where it comes from, with a link to Admin › Folder permissions; members see the count (owner and admins included) only where they can put files, and in their own folder and team project folders, never names or groups. New `GET /api/files/audience`. Five languages.
- ✦ 트리나 사이드 채팅을 열면 새 채팅으로 시작해요. The ✦ Trina side chat beside a room now opens on a new, empty chat (its starters, or the message or file you asked about) instead of the last conversation; earlier ones are in 🕘. The last chat still comes back while its answer is on its way, an answer you have not seen is waiting, the AI is asking you something, or something you typed or attached is still in its box. Switching the column to a thread and back, and moving between the room and the file viewer, keep the same chat.
- 대화방에서 여러 메시지를 복사하면 메시지만 붙여 넣어져요. Selecting several messages in a room or a thread and pressing ⌘C pasted the hover bar's ✅ 👀 👍 under every message, with reaction counts, "답글 N개", "(수정됨)", a file's size and many blank lines. It now gives one block per message: the author and time on one line (once for messages grouped under one name), the text, a file as its name, one blank line between messages. Only a message's words can be selected now; a selection inside one message copies just that text, as before. The buttons under an answer in the AI chat and the ✦ side chat (copy, send here, the model name) are no longer copied with it either. No API or data change.
- The desktop app no longer plays the room chime for a new message in the conversation on screen while its window is focused; the message just appears and the room is marked read. The page went by its own focus, which it did not have after the app started (until a click into it) and does not have while the in-app browser has the keyboard, so it rang there and left the room unread (your other open windows rang too). With the window in the back the chime still rings; with the next desktop app, also while the team list covers the page. In every app and browser, a reply in a thread shown beside a file (opened outside its room) no longer rings either. No API or data change.
- 프로젝트 멤버를 한 번에 바꿔요. A team project's members are one click away: its page has a **멤버** line (how many people see it, "9명이 봐요") with **관리**, and its card on the Projects list has **멤버** in its ⋯. The dialog shows the members now, × takes one out (struck through with **되돌리기** until you save) and the search adds one (click it to list everyone you can add); added people are marked **새로**, with their title. The edit dialog no longer holds the members. Adding someone still asks first with what they will see; the same confirm now also comes when saving would add a rule you did not choose on an older project folder (and says how many people stop seeing it). People who cannot change the members read who can ("멤버는 김소라님(만든 사람)이나 관리자가 바꿔요"). "참여 대상" is now called "멤버".
- Changing a scheduled task to **Once** now suggests today in the team's time zone. It took the UTC day, so in Jakarta before 07:00 (Seoul before 09:00) it suggested yesterday, a time that had already passed.
- A file or screenshot dragged into a room now attaches wherever you drop it: anywhere on the room's column (its header, the messages or the box) puts it in the room's box, as a drop on the box did; on a thread beside the room it goes into the thread's box, and on the ✦ side chat into the side chat's. While files are over the column one light overlay with a dashed edge says "여기에 놓으면 첨부돼요"; where the box would take nothing (it already holds 10 files, a thread that takes no reply) the overlay says why instead and the drop does nothing. The full AI chat screen takes a dropped file anywhere too (a chat in the trash, or a shared chat whose project folder you can't write to, says why). Only files from your computer: dragging text, a link, a picture or a message inside the app is left alone, pasting a screenshot into the box works as before, and a file dropped elsewhere on the page is no longer opened in place of the app. Phones are unchanged. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- 트리나 기록의 채팅에 **링크 복사**가 생겼어요. Copy link: a chat's "⋯" menu in the chat header, the sidebar's history rows, the search dialog's row menu and (new) a "⋯" on each chat row of a project page copies the chat's address on this team's address (`/c/<id>`, the address only), so you can tell teammates "I'm working here". The toast says who can open it: "링크를 복사했어요 · 프로젝트 사람 N명이 열 수 있어요" for a shared project chat, "링크를 복사했어요 · 나만 열 수 있어요" for your own chat. Copy only: nobody gains access, and a person who cannot see the chat gets the usual "not found" page, which does not say whether it exists. A signed-out person signs in and lands on the chat. No API or data change.
- In a shared project chat, someone who can only read the project folder is no longer offered **내 컴퓨터에서 올리기** (or the camera) only to be refused after picking a file: the box offers **팀 파일에서 추가**, a pasted file is not taken and a file dragged from the computer shows the not-allowed cursor. API: `Project.chatUploads` and `Chat.shared.canUpload` (optional) say whether the caller may send computer files there.
- A one-time scheduled task's day reads in your language on the Scheduled pages: "Once on Oct 29, 2026 at 17:20" (ko "2026. 10. 29.") instead of the stored "2026-10-29". The weekday of a weekly task already did. The day is never shifted by your device's time zone.
- Room header ✦ button: the label and tooltip read "Ask Trina" now, without "privately" (also in Korean, Spanish, Indonesian and Japanese). The side chat itself is unchanged.
- Admin › Usage tells removed AI connections apart. With two or more removed, the AI connection filter and the chart showed "Removed connection" for each; they are now "Removed connection 1", "Removed connection 2" (five languages). One removed connection keeps the plain name.
- The team's name and logo on top of the computer's sidebar are now the team menu: a bigger logo (28 px) and name with ▾ that open the team with its address, **Invite people** (the team invite link's message, as on Home) and **Admin**, for owners and admins. The **Admin** row is gone from the sidebar list, ⌘K finds Admin and Invite people, and the profile menu at the bottom stays yours alone. A member sees the team's name and logo without a menu. Phones keep the AI's drawer, with Admin on the 나 tab. Home's goal card **Invite teammates** now opens the invite dialog for an admin who is not the owner too, instead of sending them to the owner's setup steps and back Home. No API or data change.
- Admin › Activity log names the AI's chat naming in words: "AI used Name the chat" (five languages) instead of "AI used set_title". The step stays hidden under the answer.
- 공유 프로젝트 채팅에 내 컴퓨터의 파일을 바로 붙일 수 있어요. In a shared project chat, and in the new-chat box of a project that shares its chats, a file from your computer (📎, a paste or a drop) is now attached in one step: it is saved in the project folder's **채팅 첨부** subfolder (named in the team's language; one already there under another language's name is used), where the project's people can open it in Files, and attached as a team file. Before, the box refused it and said to upload it to the project folder first. One line under the file says where it went and how many people see it ("프로젝트 폴더(채팅 첨부)에 저장돼요 · 프로젝트 사람 3명이 볼 수 있어요"). A name that is taken gets " (2)" and nothing is overwritten; an AI instruction file's name (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, GEMINI.md, SKILL.md) is saved as "CLAUDE (2).md". It needs write access to the project folder: without it the box says so and points to **팀 파일에서 추가**. ✕ on the file before sending moves it to the team trash (restorable from Files › 휴지통). The same size and count limits and checks as any attachment apply, and it is your upload in the file's history. API: new `DELETE /api/attachments/:id`; `POST /api/uploads` and upload sessions for a shared chat answer a team-file attachment, or 403 `shared_upload_read_only`. No migration.
- A scheduled task can now save and send with your yes given once, on the card you already answer when the task is made, changed or checked again. The card gets a line or two, no new buttons: "새 파일 저장: shared/주간보고 (지금 30명이 볼 수 있어요). 넣어도 되는 내용: 내 폴더, #영업팀 대화" and "보낼 수 있는 곳: 내 Notion · '일일 메모'". Its runs then put new files in that folder (at most 20 a run) without a card, but never change or replace a file there that they did not make, never touch skills or instruction files, and save nothing there after reading a place or a room the card does not list (they save in your own folder and say why). Your own connections send only to the recipients the card pinned. When the folder is moved, renamed or deleted, its permissions change or you can no longer write there, the task says "저장 폴더를 다시 확인해 주세요" in Scheduled with **다시 확인**, you get one notice, and runs save in your own folder until then; people joining or leaving a group change nothing. The Scheduled list shows "저장: shared/주간보고" under the task, and **수정** has a "저장 폴더" field and the list of content that may go there, each removable with ×.
- The Files search says when no line has the words. A word that no file has used to show only **Related documents** (files found by meaning, which always finds some), with no "nothing found"; that label now reads "No line has “…”. Documents close in meaning:" whenever no line matches. No API or data change.
- A team file in a message that you cannot open no longer always says "You don't have access". A file that was moved or deleted after the message showed the same lock, so the words now name every reason: "Can't open: moved, deleted or no access" (five languages). It still does not say which one.
- A file name the AI chose after reading a room keeps counting as outside content when it is moved, copied, or its folder is renamed, by you or by another chat: a later chat that is shown the name still asks before it keeps a memory. A history too long to read through counts as outside too. The room card for editing a Hangul document names the file it will save as it really goes. No API or data change.
- Fewer cards after the AI read a room. In your own AI chat or the side chat beside a room, a **new file in your own folder** is saved without a card (the step says "내 폴더라 묻지 않고 저장"), also by a scheduled task that reads a room; your skills, instruction files such as CLAUDE.md, attachments and files that were there before still ask. For a new file in a **team folder**, **이번만 허용** now covers the other new files in that folder for the rest of the answer, when everyone in the rooms the chat read may write there and the chat used no web page, search or connection; a side chat that read only its own room also offers **이 방에서는 항상 허용**, the room's own allowance, listed and removed in Settings › 트리나에게 허용한 것. A file the AI saved in your folder after reading a room, the web or a connection counts as outside content when a later chat reads it, so **기억하기** asks there. The wider-audience card names the AI as the room cards do. One add-only migration (281); no API change.
- Adding a Claude subscription or ChatGPT plan from **Add account** now shows it at once, also when you close the dialog with the x, Esc or a click outside instead of **Done** (the list was reloaded only by Done), and a toast says so (in Admin › AI providers: the account was added and accounts are used in list order; elsewhere: connected). In all five languages. No API or data change.
- Fewer cards in a chat that has read a room (the ✦ side chat, or your own chat after "이 방 봐줘"). A connected service's read that the service marks read-only (a Notion or Gmail search) runs with no card, as Drive's reads do; what it sends goes to the service you or your team connected. An HTTP API's read (a GET) still asks, since some APIs change something on a GET. When what it sends holds a web address or a long random-looking string, it asks first as before. A move or copy that would put a skill or instruction file in place is still refused. A connected service's write, a scheduled task, a video, moving or copying files, moving a skill and connecting a service now ask on one card (and Trina's card for posting to a room carries the same line), their own, with the line "여기에 대화방 메시지(다른 사람이 쓴 글)가 들어와서, 무언가를 바꾸기 전에 먼저 물어봐요." on top, instead of the room card and then their own. There a team connection set to send without asking asks too, and so does a move that changes nobody's access; a connection set to refuse still refuses. Rooms, shared and confidential chats and scheduled runs are unchanged. New optional `lead` on the connect_service card. In all five languages.
- The room notification chime is about three times louder (master gain 0.12 to 0.36, same two notes and length); its mixed peak (about 0.43) stays below clipping. The on/off switch in Settings › Notifications is unchanged, and a phone in the background still uses the system notification sound, which its own volume controls. No API or data change.
- iPhone app 1.0.3: a tapped invite or team link on `*.trina.app` opens the app, at "{host}에 연결할까요?" (universal links, `applinks:*.trina.app`), and the first screen takes an invite link, a team link, a whole message with the link in it or just the team's name; the app keeps its team until another one answered and you said yes. The links open the app once `trina.app` itself serves `/.well-known/apple-app-site-association` too (the website, mobile/STORE-SUBMISSION.md); until then they open in Safari, as before.
- 트리나가 대화방에 올라온 스캔 PDF도 쪽을 이미지로 보고 읽어요. A PDF sent in a room, a DM, a group chat or a thread is now read the way a PDF in the team folder is: pages with text as text, scanned pages as pictures, so Trina no longer asks you to save a scanned PDF to the team folder first. A long PDF is read a few pages at a time, and with image viewing off (Team settings) no page goes to the AI as a picture. Trina still reads only what you can see in that conversation, checked again just before the pages go to the AI. The app connector for AI apps reads room PDFs as text, as before. No API or data change.
- The AI says why it could not do something, who can change that and where you can do it instead, and no longer blames you for something you never decided. A scheduled task that needs a confirmation now reports that nobody was asked (it said "the user did not allow this" for a card nobody saw), a confirmation that timed out is no longer read as a no, and a shared project chat says the real reason instead of "<name> did not share it". A read-only file says who can give write access (an admin, or the project's creator), an admin-only folder says an admin can do it on the Files screen, the per-answer write limit gives its number and asks you to say "continue", the daily video limit says when the next one is possible, and a connection set to refuse changes says who manages it; admins are never named, since the app shows no roles. In a room, a shared chat, a confidential chat or a scheduled run the AI knows what is not available there and points to the side panel or your own chat, and where it cannot delete it says so and points to the Files screen's trash. **move_files** and **copy_files** now make a target folder that is not there yet (the confirmation says the folder is new, in all five languages), instead of leaving a README.md you never asked for. No API or data change.
- When Trina posts to a room for you, its card now labels the note "내 이름으로 보일 한마디" (it shows under your name, outside Trina's quote), and Trina adds a note only when you asked for one; long random-looking parts of the text are highlighted. The step under the answer has **보기**, which opens the room at the message or the thread at the reply. The same text posted again after you deleted the first post goes again instead of saying it was already posted, and the audit row records `confirmed: true` once you pressed 보내기. No API break (an optional `Step.posted`). In all five languages.
- Trina can post to a room for you. Ask "이 요약 #영업팀에 올려줘" or "요약해서 스레드에 올려줘" in your own chat or a room's side chat, and one card shows the room and how many people are in it ("#영업팀(12명)에 보낼까요?", or the thread of the message you asked about), all of the text Trina wrote, and only when they apply how many people cannot see some of its sources or open a linked file; **보내기**, **파일도 함께 보내기** (when a linked file can go as a copy) or **보내지 않기**. Nothing goes without your press, also when a web page or a message tells Trina to post. It goes as your message, like **대화방에 보내기** under an answer, with the same checks; if who can see what changes while the card waits, nothing is posted and Trina asks again. In a side chat Trina posts to that room, or to the thread of the message your question quoted. Not in scheduled tasks, confidential chats, shared project chats or questions from Slack. Audited as `room.answer_share` with `via: 'ai'`. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- The AI can now do the skill-folder file chores you could only do on the Files screen, each after one card from the server: an admin can rename a project or group folder that holds skills ("projects/견적 → 2026 견적") when nobody gains or loses anything; anyone can take a file, skill or rule out of a skill folder (the card names the skills and how many people stop getting them), or put a whole SKILL.md folder of text into one (the card shows every word the AI would follow and who gets it); and a skill's folder can be renamed together with its name line. What the card showed is what happens: a file added or changed before your yes stops it. Pictures and any other file that is not text, a single rule file and instruction files still go into a skill folder only on the Files screen, where you upload them yourself, and other kinds of chat (rooms, shared chats, scheduled runs) refuse with the reason. Also: the save card for a skill counts for one save only, `make_file` asks about the file it really saves (`x.md` as Word is `x.md.docx`), moving a project or group folder records which skills stop or start applying, and dev-environment commands can no longer write a project's or your own skills.
- A file or folder the AI moves to the trash is never left out of the trash list: if its old place is taken at that moment and recording it fails, the server records it again (or reports it as moved, for an admin to recover) instead of leaving it unlisted while the AI says nothing was moved, and it never writes over what was made at the old place meanwhile. The Files screen's delete gets the same care. The audit log now says `failed` for a delete where every item failed. No API or data change.
- Asked to delete files, the AI can now move them to the team trash instead of saying it cannot: its new `delete_files` asks you first on a card that lists every file and folder in full (with how many files each folder holds), names the other people whose files are among them, says how many people will no longer see them and how many of those cannot restore them, and gives the date until which **파일 › 휴지통** keeps them (the team's trash setting; an admin can empty the trash earlier). Your yes covers exactly what the card showed: if anything in it changes before it goes (a file added, moved in, changed or removed, who can see it, the trash setting), nothing goes and the AI asks again. Only in your own AI chat, at most 200 files and folders per call (counted inside folders), at most two such cards per answer and none after a "no". Never for top-level folders, group, project or rule folders (your own projects' folders too), skill folders, other people's folders, chat attachments, plugin folders or instruction files (CLAUDE.md and the like), whatever your role; while its card waits, any other card of that answer waits behind it; not in rooms, shared project chats, chats with other people's messages, scheduled runs or confidential chats, and never through the app connector. Every call is in the audit log (`files.ai_trash`). No API or data change. In all five languages.
- One click sends a side chat's answer where it belongs. Under an answer to a question about a room message, **스레드에 보내기** (Send to thread) puts it in that message's thread at once, and its ▾ offers **대화방에 보내기** (Send to room); a follow-up such as "더 짧게" keeps the thread. Any other answer has **대화방에 보내기**. No room list, search or note any more: once sent, the button becomes "✓ 스레드에 보냈어요 · 보기" (or "대화방에 보냈어요", "앞부분만" when the answer was cut), and **보기** opens the thread at the reply or the room at the message; ✦ brings the side chat back. When some members cannot open a linked file or material the answer used, only the confirm step of the dialog opens, with the thread's message or the room on top. A thread that was deleted meanwhile says so under the answer and offers the room. Older side chats send to the room. The side chat opened as a full chat (↗) starts its 대화방에 보내기 dialog with the side chat's room picked. A question about a message now shows that message as a quote (who, when, its first lines) above the question instead of raw "> …" lines, and a new side chat is named after the question rather than the quote's header; a quick question names it "요약: <the message's first words>". API: `POST /api/chats/:id/messages/:messageId/share-to-room` takes an optional `threadRootId` (a message of that room that can have a thread; otherwise 409 `thread_root`, checked before anything else); followers of the thread are notified as for any reply. No data change. In all five languages.
- Moving or copying a skill with the AI is stricter where it could go wrong: a skill folder holding a hard-linked file is refused with what to do, a copy under a new name always loads under that name (a name like `a--b` is refused up front, and a name written over two lines in the header is rewritten as one), and a folder of the same name in other letter case counts as taken. The card lists where each file will land and says the text is cut at 65,536 characters. No API or data change.
- **Sign in with your own app** keeps its client secret closer: a new service address now drops a stored client secret also when the app's sign-in and token addresses are given (on a template too, so a second admin cannot move it elsewhere and have the members' tokens follow), and the dialog asks for it again. Entering the same client secret again, as the message about a moved token address says, now lets the next sign-in use the new address. The token address is remembered only after a sign-in that worked, a member the template is no longer for cannot start or finish a sign-in through the team's app, and an `iss` in the service's answer must match the given sign-in address's server. A secret left on a connection or template whose address an older build changed is not used. The scope hint says that with both addresses given an empty field asks for no scope (five languages). No API or data change.
- The AI can now move a skill wherever you could move it yourself on the Skills page, and copy one when you ask for a copy, instead of refusing anything that reaches more people. You decide on the server's card, which says in one line who newly gets the skill and who stops getting it ("2명이 새로 쓰고 2명은 못 쓰게 돼요 (3명 → 3명)", no longer "2 more (3 → 3)"), who newly may change it, lists every file and always shows SKILL.md (in full when someone new gets it or it leaves a project). Owners and admins, and a project's creator for that project, see the people by name; everyone else sees the counts. A copy is one card for the whole skill, every file as it is; a project copy under the name of a skill you keep at your group or the team would never run for you, so the AI asks for another name or suggests moving instead. When the AI cannot move or copy a skill it now says why and what to do (whose folder, which name, who can). The Skills page's 옮기기… uses the same wording. Copies are audited as `skills.copy` and Admin › 팀 스킬 shows where a copy came from. No API or data change.
- Trina's star in a room reads at a glance: in a message's hover bar (✦ 트리나에게 이 메시지 묻기) it no longer sits on a grey circle and is drawn larger (22 px instead of 16 px on a 20 px circle), in the room header beside 🔔 and ⋯ and in the image viewer's bar it is 24 px instead of 20 px, and in a message's ⋮ menu and its long-press sheet on a phone it has no circle either. The buttons, the hover bar and the header keep their size. The side chat's box sends with the room box's round ↑ button instead of the text button 보내기 (its name, or 답하기 while Trina waits for your answer, in the tooltip), and the line "나만 볼 수 있어요" under the box is gone: the empty side chat already says only you see it. Where the product is not named after its AI, the sparkle follows. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- The Trina side chat beside a room takes files and pictures, as the AI chat's box does: 📎, a screenshot or a file pasted into its box, or files dropped on the panel. A picture shows as itself above the box while it uploads, with ✕, and at most 20 go with a question. They are uploaded into your side chat only (your own attachments, as in any chat of yours), the answer sees them as in a full chat, and **Share to room** still sends the answer alone. No API or data change.
- On a phone, a file or photo opened from a room, a DM, a group chat or a thread now has its conversation in a sheet over it: **💬** in the top bar (with the number of replies) opens the message's thread, where you can reply, and **✦** opens your Trina side chat with the file quoted, without leaving the file. Drag the sheet's handle up for the whole screen or down to close it; Back closes the sheet first, then the file. With a file quoted in the side chat (✦ in the viewer, or **Ask Trina** in the file's preview), its box now says **이 파일에 대해 트리나에게 묻기…** instead of "about this message". On a computer, a PDF's page thumbnails show only when the space left of the thread is wide enough for them and a readable page (a 1,280 px window with the thread open shows the page wider instead), and **패널에서 열기** has its own icon (⤡) instead of a second panel icon beside the thread's toggle. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- A long PDF opens faster and uses far less memory: its preview draws only the pages near the part you are reading and releases the others as you scroll (a 60-page file opened from a room shows its first page in about two thirds of the time, with 2 or 3 pages drawn instead of 60), in the side panel, 크게 보기 and the room file viewer. Zoom and fit to width keep the page you are reading at the top, and text can still be selected and copied. In the room file viewer on a computer, a PDF of more than one page has a column of page thumbnails on its left: the page you are on is marked and follows the scroll, a click (or ↑ ↓) goes to a page, and the PDF's toolbar says where you are ("3 / 60"). No API or data change. In all five languages.
- A file or photo opened from a room, a DM, a group chat or a thread now has its conversation beside it on a computer, as in Slack: the message's thread (reply there and it is in the room's thread), or, with **✦ 트리나에게 묻기** in the viewer's bar, your Trina side chat with the file quoted. **스레드로 돌아가기** brings the thread back, the side chat stays while you step through the files, and the button before ✕ hides the column. Under a quoted file there is one more quick question, **팀 폴더에 저장**. On a phone ✦ opens the room's side chat with the file quoted. No API or data change.
- **Sign in with your own app** at services that do not let apps register themselves, such as Google's MCP servers (Gmail, Calendar, Docs, Sheets, Chat at `*mcp.googleapis.com/mcp/v1`) and APIs that sign in with OAuth, such as NAVER WORKS (`www.worksapis.com`). In the add or edit dialog of External services, an MCP server's sign-in has **내 앱으로 로그인** (the app's Client ID, Client Secret and scopes), and an API can now sign in instead of using a key, with the app's sign-in and token addresses. The dialog shows the redirect address to register at the service, with a copy button. As a template, the team's app lets each member sign in with their own account; members never see the app or its secret. The secret is stored encrypted and never shown again; a new client ID, secret, address or scope means signing in again (on a template, every member connects again). Google is asked for offline access so the sign-in lasts. An API's token is refreshed when it runs out, also after the service refuses it. Setup steps for Google and NAVER WORKS are in docs/INTEGRATIONS.md "Your own app". New optional `oauthClient` on connections and templates (input, patch and view; the view never has the secret); migration 338 (add-only). In all five languages.
- A file sent in a room, a DM, a group chat or a thread now opens over the whole window, as in Slack, instead of small in the right-hand panel. A click on its name shows a PDF's pages, a Word, PowerPoint or Excel file, notes, text, audio or video, or (with the HWP Preview plugin on) a Hangul file large in the middle, and ← → (a swipe or ‹ › on a phone) go through the conversation's pictures and files together ("3 / 7"). The top bar says who sent it, when and where (#방이름에서), with the download, **패널에서 열기** (the side panel beside the conversation) and ✕; Esc or Back closes it. A file with no preview here (a ZIP, a Hangul file with the plugin off, one too large or held by the safety check) shows a card with its name, size and the download, and a file whose message is deleted while it is open says it is no longer available. The download icon on every file still downloads at once. Files attached in the AI chat and on the Files screen still open in the side panel. No API or data change.
- The side chat starts like Slackbot: it says which room it looks at, offers **Catch up**, **Anything for me?** and **Summarize** when empty, and says that the AI can make mistakes. A file sent in a room has **Ask Trina** in its preview: the side chat opens with the file quoted (by name and id, Trina reads it) and offers **Summarize**, **Key points** and **Check it**.
- Hosted teams: a new tenant command, `owner-recovery-claimed` (`mycel-ctl owner-recovery-claimed --json` → `{ ok, cleared }`), lets the hosting service clear the recovery lock that a handover sets once the new owner has claimed recovery. `mycel-ctl reset-link` now exits 3 with `owner_recovery_locked` while that lock holds, so the hosting service can tell it from a failure. `handoverSafe` stays off until the hosting service uses it.
- @트리나 is no longer asked in a room's timeline, its threads or a DM: people ask Trina in the side chat beside the room (✦ in the room's header) and share an answer on purpose. The "@" list no longer offers the AI, and an empty room's box points to ✦. A message that starts with "@트리나" is now an ordinary message. Instead, every message has **Ask Trina about this** (✦ in its hover bar, and in its long-press menu on a phone): the side chat opens with the message quoted, with Translate, Summarize and Explain one tap away. Earlier AI answers in rooms stay as they are. An operator can bring the old way back with `"roomAiMentions": true` in server.json; `Me.server.roomAiMentions` says when it is on.
- Projects live only in `projects/`, and the people in a team choose who sees them. Any member can now make a team project (an admin can keep that to admins in Admin › Team settings › **팀원이 팀 프로젝트를 만들 수 있음**): the server makes a new `projects/<name>` folder and gives each person and group chosen, and the creator, read and write, and everyone else no access, so opening the team root to everyone does not open it. A personal project lives only in `users/<me>/projects/<name>`. The create and edit dialogs always say who can see it ("볼 수 있는 사람: 영업 부서 · 김소라 · 팀장·관리자 (9명)"); members pick people from the team list and their own groups (부서), admins any group. The creator and admins change who sees it in the edit dialog, saved like Admin › Folder permissions (a change by someone else meanwhile is refused and shown); adding people first asks once, with what they will see (files other people put there, the whole history of the project's shared chats, its project skills), and the people who already saw it are told; removing people saves at once and takes the project's shared chats away from them. Sharing a personal project makes a team project in `projects/` with the people chosen, instead of copying it into any folder. Deleting a team project can put its folder in the trash too. Removed: the **기존 폴더** kind, **프로젝트로 만들기** in Files (**프로젝트 열기** stays) and pointing a project at another folder, except an admin pointing a project whose folder is in the trash or gone at an existing `projects/<name>`. Projects made before on other folders keep working unchanged; admins see one line about them in the edit dialog. New: `GET /api/projects/:id/access`, `PUT /api/projects/:id/participants`, `POST /api/projects/access-preview`, `DELETE /api/projects/:id?trashFolder=1`, `TeamSettings.projectCreators`, `Me.team.projectCreators`; `POST /api/projects` refuses any other folder (400 `project_location`) and `POST /api/projects/:id/share` takes `participants` instead of `toParent`. Add-only migration 280 (`projects.folder_made`: only a folder the server made is its creator's to change; older projects stay the admins'). In all five languages.
- The image viewer has a **복사** button beside the download, and ⌘C / Ctrl+C in the viewer does the same: the original picture goes on the clipboard as a PNG (a JPEG, WebP or GIF too, transparency kept), to paste into a document, a mail or another chat. It uses the picture already loaded, and a copy pressed while the original loads waits for it. A picture over the 20 MB preview limit copies the smaller picture shown, and the toast says so; a toast says 이미지를 복사했어요, or why it could not. The button shows only where the clipboard takes a picture: not on a plain http page, in an older Firefox, or in the Android phone app and other Android in-app browsers (an Android WebView copies no pictures). The desktop app's right-click 이미지 복사 is unchanged. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- The team folder's top folders have names people use. Files, the folder pickers and the message box's file chips show `shared` as **전사 공유** (Company-wide), `groups` as **부서**, `projects` as **프로젝트**, `skills` as **팀 스킬**, `users` as **개인** and your own folder as **내 폴더**, at the top in that order (전사 공유, 부서, 프로젝트, 팀 스킬, 개인); the folders on disk, the API paths and what the AI sees keep their names. The Korean screens call admin groups **부서** everywhere (Admin › 부서, rules, pickers, hints); 그룹 대화 stays. The AI now knows what each top folder is for and the names you see, starts forms and quotes from `shared/양식/`, moves finished work into a 보관 folder of its area when you ask to tidy up, and gets the loose-file note for a file left directly in `projects/` too. A new team gets `shared/양식/` (in the team's language), and its setup puts 회사 소개, 브랜드, 회의록 and the first team files in `shared/` (everyone sees them) instead of the team group's folder; teams that started before keep their files where they are.
- In a PDF's preview, the toolbar's **너비에 맞추기** button now shows ↔ instead of ⤢, which is the **크게 보기** button in the panel's header right above it. No API or data change.
- **Project skills.** A team project in `projects/<name>/` can have skills of its own in its `skills/` folder (`<skill>/SKILL.md`). The AI uses them only in that project's chats (private, shared and scheduled), for the people who see the project; never in rooms or other chats. A project only adds new names (personal > group > team > project): the Skills page shows each project's section, **{project} 프로젝트 스킬** with "이 프로젝트 채팅에서만", and a small **같은 이름: 팀 전체** where a wider skill runs instead; the "/" menu marks them inside the project. Saving one asks on the same card, named for the project, and a name already taken elsewhere is offered another name instead of a save that would never run. Skills move between levels: **옮기기…** on the Skills page (who gains and who loses at each level, who may change it there; the whole text first when more people would get it) and the AI's new `move_skill`, which only moves a team skill to a group or a project, or a group's to a project, where nobody new reads it or may change it, after a card that lists every file. A project's first skill makes its skills folder. The file tools now tell the AI that skills do not move with files, instead of pointing at the Files screen (it once left a README as a stand-in). In a shared project chat a team skill everyone may use wins over a group's only some may, while a name the sender has a personal skill of stays theirs; a project skill never takes a name the sender already has. Sharing a personal project whose `skills/` holds skills (they are copied into its new `projects/<name>`), an admin pointing a project whose folder is gone at a `projects/<name>` with skills, and moving a project's folder into `projects/` on the Files screen ask first and name the skills. A scheduled task whose project its owner no longer sees is skipped, paused and says so once, instead of running outside the project. New: `GET /api/skills?projectId=` and `?projects=1`, `GET` / `POST /api/skills/move`, `skillsOk` on `POST /api/files/move`, `PATCH /api/projects/:id` and `POST /api/projects/:id/share`, optional fields on skills, skill changes, the save card's preview and inbox notices. Migration 279 (add-only). In all five languages.
- When the AI asks for several file reads and searches at once, they now run side by side (up to 4), while writes still run one at a time, in order.

## 0.5.0-beta.1 — 2026-11-09

Beta channel only; stable stays on 0.4.0.

**Upgrade note:** take a backup first (Admin › System › Backups › Back up now). This is a root update: `mycel-ctl` changed and the kit now carries the system package list (`root/packages.json`), so the update timer applies it in the team's update window, or run `sudo mycel-ctl update`; after it, a one-time package sync fetches the pinned headless browser for HTML to PDF; hosted servers also install missing system packages by themselves, while a self-hosted server installed before this release does not run apt unattended and shows what is missing until `sudo ./install-linux.sh repair`. 31 database migrations (116–118, 248–255, 257–267, 270, 272–278, 337) run by themselves at the first start. Most add tables and columns; 117 removes what deleted members left behind and switches off a team AI connection whose every assignee is gone, 252 marks existing chats as seen. Migrations 250 and 251 raise the database reader level to 6: 0.4.0 and older builds refuse the database afterwards, so going back means restoring that backup. Only four skills with a known origin ship; the welcome steps add the ones the owner picks.

- Onboarding fixes from a walk through a real build. After **팀에 준비된 것** puts text in the message box (자연스러운 글's **써 보기**, **물어보기**, the ready question) the caret now sits after the text, so what you paste or type next follows "/humanizer …" instead of landing in front of it and breaking the command; the box holding only "/humanizer 아래 글을 자연스럽게 다듬어 줘:" no longer sends, and says "다듬을 글을 붙여 넣어 주세요" under it. **물어보기** names the team folder as people read it ("예시커피 폴더에서 브랜드 폴더와 스킬 폴더는 빼고, …") instead of "groups/예시커피 … (브랜드, skills 폴더는 빼고)", and a team with nothing prepared offers "우리 팀 일을 어떻게 도와줄 수 있는지 3가지만 알려 줘" instead of "users/… 폴더에 …". The goal card of a team with no skill yet leads with a filled **스킬 넣기** ("민지님이 들어왔어요. 팀 스킬을 넣으면 같이 써 볼 수 있어요."), with **다시 보내기** beside it, quiet; **스킬 넣기** (there, on Home and on the invite step's line) opens 팀 스킬 with the ready-made skills unfolded. The ready-made skills, their source lines and the invite message name the product as the rest of the Korean screens name the AI ("트리나가 준비한 스킬", "트리나 팀이 만들었어요", "…님이 트리나에서 …"). Smaller: the line under 회사 소개's **저장하고 계속** mentions the website's photos only with a website; a phone's skill rows say **써 보기** and the ready question wraps inside its button instead of running past it; a member's empty 팀 스킬 no longer says "skills/ 폴더"; the member's skill sheet is titled **내 스킬 가져오기** like its entry. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- A conversation in the sidebar's **대화** (a team room, 전체, a DM or a group chat) can be pinned to the top, as AI chats can: its **⋯** (or a right-click, or a long press on a phone, or the room's own **⋯** at the top) has **상단에 고정**, and **고정 해제** puts it back. Pinned conversations come first, the most recently pinned first, with a small pin after the name; in the sidebar up to ten of them sit above the five latest, and **모든 대화** and the phone's **대화** tab show all of them first, right after the 트리나 row. A pin is yours only: nobody else in the room sees it. It stays across reloads and devices, your other windows follow at once, and it goes when you leave or are taken out, when the room goes, or when you hide it (a hidden conversation has no 상단에 고정; the hide's undo pins it again). Up to 50 pins. Migration 278 adds `room_pins`; new `POST` and `DELETE /api/rooms/:id/pin` and `Room.pinnedAt`. In all five languages.
- On a computer, the panel beside the AI chat and the Files screen (a file preview or an artifact) has a **크게 보기** button (⤢) left of its ✕: the panel then fills the space beside the sidebar and the chat or the file list behind it is hidden, so a PDF, a document or a sheet reads at full size. **나란히 보기** (⤡) or Esc brings it back beside the chat at the width you had set; a second Esc closes it. It stays big while the panel is open, also when you open another file from a link in it, and the next panel opens side by side. **Add to chat**, **Ask AI** and **Show in Files** go back side by side so you see where the file went, and moving to another page does too. Phones have no button: the panel is full screen there already.
- A Hangul file (HWP, HWPX) sent in a room, a DM or a thread now opens as pages in the side panel, as a team file does, while the team has the HWP Preview plugin on: a click on its name opens it, 이전 and 다음 turn the pages, and the download is still there. The same checks as opening the file apply (only people who can see the message, never a file held by the safety check), with the team preview's renderer and limits. With the plugin off or not added, nothing changes: the file downloads on a click. New: `GET /api/rooms/:id/files/:fileId/hwp-preview` and `Me.server.hwpPreview`.
- A room has a private side chat with @트리나 (✦ Trina). The ✦ button in the room's header opens it beside the room, where a thread opens (the whole screen on a phone). Only you see it. Trina knows the room and reads it as you see it, so "summarize this discussion", "make to-dos from the meeting" or "translate Mia's message" work there. Nothing goes to the room until you press **Share to room** under an answer: the existing "send to a chat room" dialog opens with this room picked, and checks the files and material some members cannot see. The side chat is an ordinary chat of yours (it is also in your chat history), so "Open as a full chat" continues it there. 🕘 lists the room's side chats, the chat history shows the room next to a side chat's title, and while Trina waits for the answer to its question the panel's box answers it. @트리나 in the room itself works as before for now. Migration 276 adds `chats.side_room_id`; `Chat.sideRoomId`, `CreateChatBody.sideRoomId` and `ListChatsQuery.sideRoomId` are new optional fields.
- A chat in the sidebar's **트리나 기록** can be pinned to the top: its **···** (or a right-click, or a long press on a phone) has **상단에 고정**, and **고정 해제** puts it back. Pinned chats come first, the most recently pinned first, with a small pin before the title; on a computer up to ten of them sit above the five latest chats, and **모두 보기** and the phone's list show all of them first. A project's page lists your pinned chats there first too. A pin is yours only: on a shared project chat nobody else sees it. It stays across reloads and devices, your other windows follow at once, and it goes when the chat is deleted or you can no longer see it. Up to 50 pins. Migration 277 adds `chat_pins`; new `POST` and `DELETE /api/chats/:id/pin`, `Chat.pinnedAt`, and the first page of `GET /api/chats` starts with your pins. In all five languages.
- A picture clicked anywhere in the app now opens over the whole window, as in Slack, instead of small in the right-hand panel: a photo in a room or a thread, a picture attached to an AI chat, one in an answer, one in either message box, and the picture in a file's preview panel. It fits the window (never drawn larger than itself), shows its thumbnail at once and then the original. A click zooms to 100 % about that point (2× for a small picture), ⌘/Ctrl + scroll or a trackpad pinch zooms about the pointer, a drag pans, and + − 0 and the bar's − / % / + step. ← → and the arrows at the edges go through the pictures of the room, the thread or the chat on screen ("3 / 7"). The bar shows the name, who sent it and when, the download, the original in a new tab (in a browser) and ✕; Esc, ✕, a click beside the picture and Back close it. On a phone: pinch, double tap, a swipe sideways to the next picture and a swipe down to close; a tap hides the bar. In the desktop app 이미지 복사 copies the original. A file's name, chip or link still opens the panel. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- The AI chat can now save a file sent in a conversation into the team folder as it is (a PDF, a picture, any file). "leads 방에 올린 사업자 등록증, 다들 볼 수 있게 저장해줘" asks once, on one card that names the room, the file, who sent it and where it goes, and saves the same file there; before, the AI could only read it as text and asked for it to be attached again. You must be able to see the file in the room; a file held by the team's safety check, a folder you cannot write, skills/ and instruction files are refused, and a file already there is kept with the copy saved next to it. In a room, @트리나's copy of a room file now asks one card instead of two when it goes where more people can see it. No API or data change.
- A picture pasted or attached in a message box (a room's and the AI chat's) now shows as a 64 px square of itself above the text, as Slack and iMessage show one, instead of a 32 px dot in a file chip. Its ✕ sits on the corner (a 44 px tap on phones), a spinner sits over it while it uploads (the AI chat's percent beside the spinner), and a failed one gets a red edge with the reason beside it. The name shows on hover; in the AI chat a click on the picture opens it, as a click on its name did. Three fit one row on a phone. Other files keep their compact chip. No API or data change.
- The Mac desktop app's Dock icon now shows a dot when a room has unread messages that are not for you, such as a plain message in a team room (the sidebar's count), as Slack and Discord do. The number still counts what is for you (DMs and group chats, mentions, followed threads, AI chats that need you) and wins over the dot; rooms set to 끄기 or hidden never light it, and it clears once they are read. An installed web app's icon does the same where the browser shows a dot (the Mac Dock, the Windows taskbar). The desktop bridge's `setBadgeCount` takes a `dot` flag (older desktop apps ignore it). No API or data change.
- A scheduled task's card now names only the connected services the task needs. Before, every card listed every team connection set to send without asking ("이 연결 서비스는 AI가 묻지 않고 보내도록 설정돼 있어요: …") with three buttons, even for a news briefing. Now a service is on the card only when the task names it (in its title or instruction, or the AI lists it when it makes the task); otherwise the card has the plain 만들기 / 만들지 않기 and the task's runs send to no connected service. The same holds for 수정 and 다시 확인 in Settings › Scheduled tasks; an edit's card also offers again the services the task could already send to, and plain 바꾸기 drops them. Tasks made before keep what they were allowed. No API or data change.
- The small menus on a computer (a row's or a card's **…**, a room's **⋯** and bell, a message's **⋮**) stay inside the window. A sidebar room row's **Notification settings** (wider than the sidebar in English) ran off the window's left edge and cut the first words of each choice ("…ult (from settings)"); it now moves right, and every such menu keeps 8 px from the window's edges, opens above its button near the bottom, and wraps its words only when the window is narrower than the menu. It is no longer cut by the sidebar's scrolling list; it follows its row when the list scrolls and closes once the row is out of sight. Phones keep the bottom sheet. No API or data change.
- The Inbox's own notices now read like its conversation rows. A scheduled task's notice names the task (**"주간 보고" 예약 작업이 끝났어요**; also when it could not finish, finished with problems or needed your own key), with its time on the right as the message rows have it, and the whole row opens the result chat; the small **결과 열기** link and the date line under it are gone. The other notices (a dev environment job, a connection, a team skill, a member, …) work the same way: the row opens its page, an unread one has a dot where a message row has its count, and opening it reads it. A notice with nowhere to go is not a link and keeps **읽음 표시**. A deleted task, and a server from before this, keep the old "예약 작업이 완료됐어요". The title is read from the task when the Inbox is read, never stored with the notice, and never shown on a lock screen. API: `InboxNotification.schedule` (`{ title }`), additive.
- Room notifications have a **Mark as read** button (읽음으로 표시) in the browser and installed web app (Chrome, Edge, Android; Safari shows no buttons) and in the Mac desktop app. It marks the room, or the thread for a reply, read up to that message, as opening it would, without opening the app. Windows, the iPhone and Android apps show no button yet. The single notice a push opens now carries the message's `seq`, `/api/push` gives the button's words (`messages.markRead`), and the desktop bridge has `notify({ read })` and `onRead` (older apps show no button).
- Room notifications can show the message. A new switch, Settings › Notifications › **메시지 미리보기 표시** (on by default, as in Slack), puts the message's first line in the notification's body on the computer (desktop app), in the browser and installed web app (Web Push), and on an iPhone with the 1.0.1 app. Off: who and where only, with the generic line, as before. A private @트리나 answer and a message with only files always show the generic line. The push gateway still gets ids only: the device fetches the line from its own team server after sign-in. Migration 275 adds `notification_preferences.message_preview`; `NotificationPreferences.messagePreview` and `RoomAlertSettings.messagePreview` are new optional fields.
- In the desktop app a right-click now opens a small menu: on a photo **이미지 복사** and **이미지 저장…** (copying from the preview panel copies the original), on a link **링크 복사**, on selected text **복사**, and in a text box **잘라내기 / 복사 / 붙여넣기 / 모두 선택** (also in the app's own first screen, for pasting an invite link). Before, a right-click did nothing in the app. In the browser, its own menu (Copy Image) keeps working. A picture pasted into a room's message box now shows as a picture above the text at once, while it uploads, with its ✕, as in the AI chat's box. No API or data change. In all five languages.
- A picture with a transparent background (a seal stamp, a logo) sent in a room or attached to a chat no longer shows on a black square: its thumbnail is now a PNG that keeps the transparency, and once it has loaded nothing is painted behind it, in light and dark mode. Other pictures keep their JPEG thumbnails. Thumbnails of transparent pictures sent before are made again once, in the background, a minute after the update. No API or database change.
- A new member's first Home shows what the team prepared. Under the box, **팀에 준비된 것** lists up to three team skills (each with its one line, **예시** for a ready-made one and **써 보기**; 자연스러운 글 puts "/humanizer 아래 글을 자연스럽게 다듬어 줘:" in the box for the member's own text) until they have run one, and the team folder's documents with **물어보기**. With nothing prepared, **이렇게 시작해 보세요** offers one question to send. Owners and admins see the same rows without a filled button, or **스킬 넣기 · 팀 파일 넣기** on an empty team. The invite step says once when members would find nothing yet ("지금 초대하면 팀원이 볼 게 없어요"), with the way back to those steps. The folder card and the three example questions are gone, and 「자주 쓰는 스킬」 stays off Home while the team's skills are listed there. New endpoint: `GET /api/skills/examples`.
- Team servers now serve a real `/.well-known/assetlinks.json` (it was 404): it names the app `app.trina.ax` and the SHA-256 of the upload key that signs the Android APK from updates.trina.app, so Android can verify app links for it. Google Play's app signing key must be added to the same list once Play signs the app. No other change.
- On a computer, the AI chat's row at the top of the rooms list (대화) now shows @트리나's face from its rooms, Trina's star on the neutral circle, instead of the app icon. The sidebar's team logo is unchanged; an edition whose AI is not named after the product shows the sparkle there. No API or data change.
- The Korean screens name past AI chats **기록**, as the sidebar does: the project page's list is **트리나 기록** (the AI's history in other languages too), and lines about past chats, the trash setting and scheduled runs say 기록 (기록 열기). Confidential mode stays **기밀 대화**, and Admin's **기록** page is now **활동 로그**. A chat row's **···** is the same menu as a room row's: a right-click opens it, and on a phone a long press opens it as a sheet. A room's **⋯** has **이름 바꾸기** for people who may rename it, and **대화방 나가기** is no longer repeated in the room's info. Unused strings and an unused settings page are removed. No API or data change.
- 팀장 넘기기 is easy, and leaves whoever set the team up no hidden access. The first account still owns the team and nobody is asked at sign-up. On the invite (the welcome steps' 팀원 초대 and Admin › 멤버's invite form) the owner can tick **이 사람이 팀장이에요** and say who it is for (**누구에게 보내나요?**, up to 60 characters): the link is for one person, makes a member in the team's group, works for 3 days, and a new one stops the one before. When that person joins, the owner gets one card at the top of Home, an Inbox notice and a phone, browser or desktop notification, showing the owner's own words beside the account's name, username and join time, with **넘기기** and ✕ (✕ hides the card; the notice stays). The flag changes no role: only the owner's **넘기기** with the password does, so a forwarded link gives a stranger a member's seat any admin can disable. The confirm (from the card, the Inbox or Admin › 멤버 › ··· › **팀장 넘기기**, which replaces the card in Admin › 팀 설정) says who receives it and what moves, lists the connections made with the owner's own accounts (a team Claude or ChatGPT sign-in, Drive shared with the team; the team's API keys are not listed), each ticked to be ended after the handover, and who can still reset passwords outside the app. Afterwards the new owner's Inbox says the old owner stays an admin, with **내보내기**, the old owner and the other admins are told, and both people's open windows switch role at once. Two handovers at once can no longer make two owners (the second gets "이미 팀장이 바뀌었어요"). The person receiving it must have changed their temporary password and signed in once. Five wrong passwords in 15 minutes lock the check and tell the owner in the Inbox. Hosted teams keep only ··· › 팀장 넘기기 until the hosting service refuses password-reset links for owners and admins after a handover (`handoverSafe` in server.json). Migration 270 (add-only). In all five languages.
- @트리나's card in a room (확인해 주세요) comes back after a reload, in another browser, on the phone and after a reconnect. Before, the room showed only "트리나가 답하는 중" and the sidebar's "?", with nothing to answer, until the card timed out. Only the person who asked gets the card; the others still see only that the AI is answering. A new card now comes fully into view when you are at the bottom of the room (it could stay below the message box with only its first line showing); if you scrolled up to read, the room stays where you are. The cards' buttons are in one order, the main one last, on the right: **허용 안 함**, **이 방에서는 항상 허용**, **이번만 허용** (the address and dev-environment cards the same); on a phone the three stack with the main one on top, and the keyboard and screen readers meet them in that order (main first). New: `GET /api/rooms/:id/ai/requests` (each card's turn, message and question; no chat id). No data change.
- Deleting a project no longer deletes its shared chats for good: each goes to its creator's **삭제한 기록**, as when one shared chat is deleted, and comes back from there as their own chat without a project (other people's turns in it show as they did while it was shared). The confirm says so in plain words instead of the red line. A project editor who deletes someone else's shared chat now finds it in their own 삭제한 기록 too, while they can still bring it back, and the delete confirm names that screen by its real name. Someone who loses access to a project now sees its shared chats leave their chat list at once, and an open project page shows a chat someone else starts, renames or deletes without a reload. New add-only migration (`chats.deleted_by`).
- A room's private @트리나 answer now asks whether to post it, also when it used an earlier answer some people there cannot see. Before, such an answer said "대화방에 올릴 수 없어요" and stayed with you. Now the notice says in one plain line why only you see it ("방 사람 중 일부가 볼 수 없는 자료를 참고한 답이라 지금은 나만 보고 있어요."), and **방에 올리기…** first shows what is at stake: each folder by name with how many people in the room cannot see it ("단가표 · 방 사람 중 2명은 못 봐요"), never who, and a line when it went through an earlier hidden answer, whose own sources are named. 취소 leaves it private; 방에 올리기 posts it as before. Every private answer asks this way now, not only those. It still cannot be posted, and says so with no button, when the earlier answer used something you cannot open yourself (another conversation, a shared project chat, a connection, the web, someone else's folder, a folder closed to you) or when a room member's history is still being worked out. Answers kept private this way before this release are checked again in the background after the update, and when someone leaves the room. If the reasons changed meanwhile (someone joined, a folder rule changed), the app says so and shows the new ones first. Threads work the same way.
- The four ready-made skills are chosen by name. The setup step 내 스킬 now reads **팀이 쓸 스킬을 골라 주세요** and lists 회사 소개서, 카드뉴스, 홍보 영상 and 자연스러운 글 (only the ones the team does not have), each with one line and **예시**; the first three come checked when the company profile was saved, 자연스러운 글 never, and no AI picks. Bringing your own Claude Code or Codex skills is under them, with the one-line way behind one fold, and one button names what goes in, your own skills too, up to three ("회사 소개서·카드뉴스·홍보 영상 넣기", "카드뉴스·주간 업무 보고 넣기", then "팀에 넣기 · 5개"); when anything does not go in, the step says why (a name taken, folder rules, a request to try again) instead of moving on. On the owner's press, 회사 소개서, 카드뉴스 and 홍보 영상 first put the website's pictures and 회사 소개.md in the team's brand folder when they are not there yet. The AI's check of your own skills now really ends after 30 seconds: the list then shows with nothing ticked, and a late answer ticks nothing (`POST /api/skill-shares/:id/stop-check`). On the Team skills page the empty page's **추천 스킬 4개 넣기** becomes one folded line under the list for owners and admins, **트리나가 준비한 스킬 · N개 더**, with nothing ticked and **팀에 넣기 · n개**; it is hidden while you search and gone when the team has them all, and a skill a later version ships only raises its count, with no notice. **내 스킬 가져오기** is there for everyone on a computer: owners and admins pick a folder and add the skills, members suggest them; a phone says it is done on a computer. **예시** opens one window per skill (a sheet on phones) with an example made for an invented company and where the skill comes from; only the English 자연스러운 글 links out, to its MIT original. The examples were made by the skills themselves for the invented coffee roaster 예시커피 / Example Coffee, in Korean and English (teams in other languages see the English ones): the deck's cover and two slides, three of the five cards, three frames of the 18-second video, and for 자연스러운 글 an AI-sounding notice or post and its rewrite. The pictures (about 30 KB each) load only when the window opens. `GET /api/onboarding/packs` is new and `POST /api/onboarding/packs/:id/install` takes `skills`. No database migration. In all five languages.
- A tapped invite or team link opens the app. On a phone, links on `*.trina.app` open the phone app (universal links and app links), and the join pages have "앱에서 열기", which opens the desktop app on a Mac or Windows PC and the Android app (an intent that only our app can receive) on any team server, including one installed by the company itself; iPhones get none, since iOS lets any app claim a link scheme. Every such link first shows "{host}에 연결할까요? 직접 받은 링크가 맞을 때만 연결하세요." (with "트리나가 운영하는 주소가 아니에요…" for addresses Trina does not run, and on a phone in another team what switching means), and nothing loads before 연결. Team servers now answer `/.well-known/apple-app-site-association` and `/.well-known/assetlinks.json`.
- Getting into a team is one box. The desktop and phone apps' first screen ("팀에 들어가요") takes the invite link or team link you got, a whole KakaoTalk or Slack message with the link in it, a full address or just the team's name (`acme`), and opens that link's page inside the app: a pasted invite or team link no longer loses its token on the desktop, and the phone now takes team links and bare names. Typing in 한글 mode, an email address or a cut-off link each get their own hint, and a name the relay does not know says "'acme' 팀을 찾지 못했어요" (a suspended team says so too, instead of "not a team server"). "주소를 모르겠어요" and "아직 팀이 없어요" explain where the address is and who makes a team; phones and the Mac App Store build have no signup button there (store rules), only the install guide. The apps show the team address they connected to above the sign-in pages (a page cannot fake it), ask once before connecting to an address Trina does not run, and the phone keeps its team until another one answered and you said yes ("{old}에서 나가고 {new}에 연결할까요?"). The desktop's 팀 menu has one "팀 목록·추가…". On the web, Settings › 앱 shows the team address with a copy button, a used or expired link leads with [로그인], and both join forms end with "이미 가입했어요? 로그인". The relay keeps `acme`, `trina` and `trina-ax` for itself.
- The team address line no longer makes the page jump: off the sign-in pages the desktop strip and the phone footer wait a moment for the team's answer, so a signed-in open never shows them and hides them again. Links from outside no longer reach this machine through an IPv4-mapped or unspecified address (`[::ffff:127.0.0.1]`, `[::]`, `0.0.0.0`); a pasted address with a word glued on (`https://acme.trina.app입니다`) reads as the address; and the phone's start no longer runs two device removals side by side, which on a slow network could forget the removal still owed to a team it left.
- Search (⌘K) finds messages in your conversations: team rooms, the everyone room, 1:1 and group chats. Type a word and the All tab shows **대화방 메시지** after your chat records: who wrote it (their picture), where ("#디자인 리뷰", "1:1 대화", a group chat's name, "· 스레드" for a reply), when, and one line of the message with the word marked; the first five, then **메시지 더 보기**. Part of a word works ("견적" finds "견적서를"). Choosing one opens the conversation at that message, marked for a moment, also when it is far back: the conversation then shows the part around it, loads newer messages as you scroll down, and **최근 메시지로** (or sending) goes back to the newest; a reply opens its thread. Only what you would see by opening that conversation now is found: nothing from conversations you are not in or have left, nothing from before you joined a group chat or a 1:1, never an earlier answer hidden from you ("볼 수 없는 자료를 참고한 답이라 가려졌어요") or its thread, nothing deleted or edited away. Inbox links to an older message open it the same way. The search box now reads "기록·대화 메시지, 파일, 프로젝트 등 검색". New `GET /api/room-messages`, and `GET /api/rooms/:id/messages` takes `around`. One database migration (267, additive: a search index of the messages, filled once at the first start, about 2–3 seconds per 100,000 messages). On computers and phones, in all five languages.
- Shared project chats now follow the rooms' rule for people who join later. An answer that used something you cannot open, from before you could see the chat, shows one faint line in its place ("볼 수 없는 자료를 참고한 답이라 가려졌어요") instead of the whole answer; the people who saw the chat when the AI read it, and anyone who can open what it read, still see it, and the person who asked always does. Nothing of a hidden answer reaches you elsewhere either: not in search, the files it used, its artifacts, an export, @트리나 in a room, or the AI when you continue the chat. Earlier answers keep showing to the people who see the chat now. A group's skills in a shared chat are now for the group's members only, as in rooms: the AI is not shown them while someone who sees the chat is not in the group (the owner and admins can read every group's folder), and using one asks first ("…명은 이 스킬을 쓸 수 없어요").
- Slack: **Post to channel** now checks what the answer used. When it used places not everyone on the team can see (a group's folder, your own folder, a team connection or shared Drive, the in-app browser, your personal connections, a room), the first press shows you a notice naming those places (never what is in them or who cannot see them) with **Post anyway** and **Cancel**; Post anyway checks your link and Slack Connect again and posts once, Cancel puts the answer back to you only. Slack does not say who in a channel is who here, so the test is the whole team. File names a listing showed count too. Your personal memories do not count. Answers from what everyone reads post at once as before; an answer from a confidential (safe operations) conversation is never posted; DMs are unchanged. No API or database change. In all five languages.
- The account menu no longer has **Appearance**. The theme (system, light, dark) is chosen in Settings, one place for it. No API or data change.
- A deleted team skill now opens 파일 › 휴지통, where the file is. On 팀 스킬 › 최근 변경 a 삭제 row's button read "삭제한 기록" and, like the notice to owners and admins, opened the deleted AI chats; it now reads 휴지통 and opens the files trash, which has its own address (`/files?view=trash`), so Back and a reload keep the tab. Also: @트리나's answer that only the asker sees now counts as unread for them at once, on the room in the sidebar (and wherever that room's count shows), not only after a reload.
- Usage limits now count by the team's time zone everywhere, also on a server whose clock runs in another zone (hosted servers run in UTC). A daily or monthly budget tells the admins once in each of the team's days or months: before, a Korean team's daily budget used up again before 09:00 could stay silent while the person was already blocked. An AI connection's monthly limit, its "this month" figure and the time it comes back follow the team's month like the Usage page: it starts again at 00:00 on the team's 1st (for a Korean team it was 09:00). The limit's hint in Admin › AI connections says team time zone. Right after the update a budget notice may come once more. No API or database change. In all five languages.
- @트리나 in a room can save new team files without asking every time. Its card now offers **이번만 허용**, **이 방에서는 항상 허용** and **허용 안 함**, with one line under them: "내가 부를 때, 이 폴더에 새 파일을 만들 때만 적용돼요." "이번만" on such a card covers the rest of that answer for the folder; "항상" lets your later questions in that room make new files there (and change files the same answer made) without the card, for 30 days. It is offered only for new files, in a folder below the top folders that everyone in the room may write, when the question used nothing from the web, never for skills or instruction files; edits of existing files, moves, renames, downloads and anything else still ask every time. A grant stops working when someone joins the room, who may read or write the folder changes, you leave the room or 30 days pass. The room's answer lists the files saved this way. A save that also needs the wider-audience card now shows that one card, with what it writes, instead of two. Saving from a team room to its group's folder no longer asks about the wider audience only because the owner or admins (who can add themselves to the group and see its history) are not in the room. Every server card is titled "확인해 주세요", and the room card says what the AI will do and that changed files can be restored from their history. Settings › 트리나에게 허용한 것 lists your grants with 지우기; admins can turn the choice off in Team settings ("대화방에서 '항상 허용' 쓰기", on by default). Migration 266 (additive). In all five languages.
- Self-hosted servers: the server log now warns at start-up when a database ran a migration whose file was renamed later, naming the recorded and the current file, so a skipped migration shows. The server still starts. CI now fails on a migration file the server would skip.
- AI apps connected to the team (Claude Code, Codex, ChatGPT and others): what the AI read now counts per session, as each chat in the app does. Before, one read of a private file kept every later save to a shared folder refused for as long as the connection lasted (up to 30 days), in every new session, without saying why. Now a new session starts with nothing read; an app that keeps no sessions forgets what it read after an hour without using the connection. The refusal says what to do: save in a folder with the same or narrower access, or start a new session. `write` and `edit` now say why a save was refused (a read-only folder, someone else's folder), as `upload_file` does, and the refusal is in the file log. The connected AI also learns which team rules (`skills/*.md` without a skill header) and company profile it may read: the connection's instructions name them and `skills_list` marks the rule files `rule: true`. Migration 118 (add-only). No screen change.
- Which AI answers is decided in one place. A scheduled task now runs on the engine and model its owner would get for a chat they start then (their own default engine when they may pick one, else the team default), not always on the team default: someone with Codex as their default and only a ChatGPT connection no longer gets every scheduled run failing with "no AI connection". Team settings' "Claude 한도에 닿으면 Codex로 넘기기" is now **다른 AI로 대신 답하기**: when a chat's AI has no working connection of the person, or Claude hits its usage limit, the other connected AI (Claude Code or Codex) answers instead, and the answer says so; the warning now names both companies (OpenAI or Anthropic). The first case used to depend on the hosting service's trial key being on the server, so a team whose key was cleared, or that moved to its own server, lost it with no setting changed. Self-hosted servers keep the switch as it was (off unless an admin turned it on). Hosted servers turn it on once with this update, which also turns on the Claude limit case for them; an admin can turn it off and it stays off. No API or database change. In all five languages.
- Deleting a group or a member no longer leaves things behind. A team AI connection assigned only to that group or person is switched off instead of serving nobody, and never becomes the whole team's; the delete confirmation names it in one line ("이 그룹만 쓰던 AI 연결이 꺼져요: …"), and editing a connection that still named a deleted group no longer fails. The AI connection dialogs no longer save "그룹·사람 고르기" with nobody ticked (that meant the whole team); they ask for at least one. A deleted person's photo, title and status and their own AI connections (token or key) are deleted with the account. Moving a deleted member's personal folder to the owner's archive now works like a move on the Files screen: version history, projects and the text cache go with it. Migration 117 (data only) does the same for earlier deletes. In all five languages.
- 팀 파일 in the welcome steps recommends from a folder. Pick a folder with company documents (Chrome, Edge and the desktop app open the folder window; Safari and Firefox the folder picker) and the page lists its documents without opening them: up to 5,000 documents, 6 folders down, 15 seconds; never hidden files, app bundles, node_modules, .git or Library; files over 50 MB are only counted. The AI picks what the team should have from names, folders, types, sizes and dates only, never content, and never sees a file whose path looks personal (급여, 인사, 이력서 …). One list follows: the AI's top 20 ticked with a reason word (양식, 회사 소개, 회의록 …), "이미 있어요" for what is already in the folder, the personal-looking files apart and unticked, "다른 파일도 보기" with a search, "폴더 더 고르기" to add another folder. Nothing goes up before **추천 N개 올리기** (at most 100 at a time). The upload is a folder upload of the file screen: it goes on on other pages, the step comes back to it (never to its start), and picking the same files again makes no "(1)" copies. Without AI, or after 30 seconds, the list shows unticked. Phones upload hand-picked files. After setup, owners and admins see "폴더에서 회사 문서 추천받기" on the Files screen while the team folder has no file.
